  if (this.ai0 >= 45) { this.dead = true; return; }
  // 六角尘+光(:30810-30821)
  const va = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);
  const [rX, rY] = rot2(0, -720, va);    // (0,-720).RotatedBy(va)
  const k = (this.ai0 % 45) / 45;
  const sx = rX * k, sy = rY * k;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    const [hx, hy] = rot2(sx, sy, i * Math.PI * 2 / 6);
    const px = this.cx + hx, py = this.cy + hy;
    game.addLightAt?.(px, py, 0.3, 0.75, 0.9);
    for (let d = 0; d < 2; d++) {
      game.spawnDust?.(px + (Math.random()*8-4), py + (Math.random()*8-4), 8, 8, 197, 0, 0, 100, null, 1, true);
    }
  }
  this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;  // 引擎速度积分(:19026) → 与 AI 反漂抵消,子体净静止
}
// 接触伤害(penetrate -1 不消耗)
hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);
hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, 0, 'hostile');
hitCritters(this, game);
```

Hmm — hitCritters each tick would insta-kill overlapping critters (fine, that's vanilla-ish for a piercing hostile... vanilla 464 penetrate -1 = infinite). OK.

Order check for child: kill at ai0>=45 BEFORE dust — vanilla order: alpha update, kill check, dust. ✓ matches my sketch.

Draw for mother: projSprite(464) rotated by this.rot, alpha (255-alpha)/255, at center.
Draw for child: Extra_35 hex sprites (:32699-32721): NO Projectile_464 sprite (skip default draw); for i in 0..5: frame band cycling (band = i % 3? — the code: rectangle21 starts Frame(1,3) = first third (0..74); after each draw rectangle21.Y += height; wraps to 0 when >= 222 → so corner i uses band i%3); rotation = i*2π/6 + velocity angle + π; position = center + spinningpoint7.RotatedBy(i*2π/6). So:

```ts
const img = extra35Sprite();
const bandH = img.height / 3;
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
  const ang = i * Math.PI * 2 / 6;
  const [hx, hy] = rot2(sx, sy, ang);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(this.cx + hx, this.cy + hy);
  ctx.rotate(ang + va + Math.PI);
  ctx.globalAlpha = (255 - this.alpha) / 255;
  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, (i % 3) * bandH, img.width, bandH, -img.width/2, -bandH/2, img.width, bandH);
  ctx.restore();
}
```

73:function frameOf(type: number): { fx: number; fy: number } {
118:    const f = frameOf(type);
function frameOf(type: number): { fx: number; fy: number } {
  let fx = 10 * type, fy = 10 * (Math.random() * 3 | 0);
  let t = type;
  while (t >= 100) { t -= 100; fx -= 1000; fy += 30; }
  return { fx, fy };
}

export class VanillaDust {
  private pool: VDust[] = [];
  private cursor = 0;
  /** update 期收集的光照（Game 每帧推 LightingEngine 后清空） */
  lights: Array<{ x: number; y: number; r: number; g: number; b: number }> = [];

  ...
}

But is the tsconfig set with noImplicitOverride? Using override when not needed is fine. k < 2; k++) {\n          game.spawnDust?.(hex[i][0] + (Math.random() * 8 - 4), hex[i][1] + (Math.random() * 8 - 4),\n            8, 8, 197, 0, 0, 100, null, 1, true);   // 尘 197 noGravity（:30817-30820）\n        }\n      }\n      this.x += this.vx;                                           // 引擎速度积分（:19026）\n      this.y += this.vy;\n    }\n    // 接触伤害（hostile penetrate=-1，命中不消耗；敌弹 ×2 缩放在 hitPlayer 内）\n    hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);\n    hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, 0, 'hostile');\n    hitCritters(this, game);\n  }\n\n  /** 六角波纹角点（中心+偏移）：(0,−720).RotatedBy(速度角)×(ai0%45/45) 再各旋 k·60° */\n  private hexPoints(): Array<[number, number]> {\n    const va = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);\n    const [rx, ry] = rot(0, -720, va);\n    const k = (this.ai0 % 45) / 45;\n    const sx = rx * k, sy = ry * k;\n    const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {\n      const [hx, hy] = rot(sx, sy, (i * Math.PI * 2) / 6);\n      pts.push([this.cx + hx, this.cy + hy]);\n    }\n    return pts;\n  }\n\n  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const bright = (255 - this.alpha) / 255;\n    if (bright <= 0) return;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    ctx.globalAlpha = bright;\n    if (this.mother) {\n      // Projectile_464.png × rotation（Main.cs:32360 大表分支默认画法）\n      const img = projSprite(464);\n      if (img && img.width > 0) {\n        ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);\n        ctx.rotate(this.rot);\n        ctx.drawImage(img, -img.width / 2, -img.height / 2, img.width, img.height);\n      } else {\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#9FD8E8';\n        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(this.cx, this.cy, 24, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();\n      }\n    } else {\n      // 六角波纹 Extra_35（Main.cs:32699-32721）：角点 i 用第 i%3 帧带、\n      // 旋角=角点角+速度角+π；不画 464 贴图\n      const img = extra35();\n      const va = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx);\n      const hex = this.hexPoints();\n      for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {\n        const ang = (i * Math.PI * 2) / 6;\n        ctx.save();\n        ctx.translate(hex[i][0], hex[i][1]);\n        ctx.rotate(ang + va + Math.PI);\n        if (img && img.width > 0) {\n          const band = img.height / 3;\n          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, (i % 3) * band, img.width, band,\n            -img.width / 2, -band / 2, img.width, band);\n        } else {\n          ctx.fillStyle = '#BFE8F0';\n          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 8, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();\n        }\n        ctx.restore();\n      }\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}\n\n/** 闪电球 465（Projectile.cs aiStyle 88 type 段 :30860-30937 + SetDefaults :5109-5118）：\n *  邪教徒闪电态(:65563-65566)在头顶 100px 零速定点生成（原版 NewProjectile 速度 0——\n *  本仓旧实现曾坍缩为 6 速直飞 Arrow，实体化后还原定点）。\n *  80×80 / hostile / alpha 255 / tileCollide=false（无 timeLeft 覆写，AI 自灭）。\n *  生命周期：ai0<180 淡入 alpha−5/t（钳 0）；≥180 淡出 +5/t，≥255 Kill（:30870-30887）。\n *  每逢 ai0%30==0 且 <180（:30889-30917）：对 ≤5 个 <2000px 且 Collision.CanHit\n *  （1×1 点对）的玩家各掷一颗 466 闪电链（LightningBoltProj）——方向=弹心→玩家心\n *  RotatedByRandom(0.785) 归一 ×7 速、ai0=该方向角、ai1=Next(100) 种子。\n *  4 帧循环（frameCounter≥4 推帧，projFrames[465]=4，:30919-30925）；AddLight\n *  (0.4,0.85,0.9)；alpha<150 且 ai0<180 时每子步 2 圈尘 226（半径 w×0.2/0.6，\n *  速度=离心 2 / 0，:30929-30960）。首帧 Item_121 音（:30864-30867）保留在教徒\n *  调用点（任务约定不双播）。接触伤害=传入 damage（教徒闪电 45）。 */\nexport class LightningOrb extends Entity {\n  readonly projId = 465;\n  damage: number;\n  w = 80; h = 80;                  // SetDefaults :5110-5111\n  private ai0 = 0;\n  private alpha = 255;\n  private frameCounter = 0;\n  private frame = 0;               // projFrames[465]=4（vanilla-projframes.json）\n\n  /** x/y = NewProjectile 的 X/Y（中心锚 = 教徒 Center −(0,100)）；速度恒 0 */\n  constructor(x: number, y: number, damage: number) {\n    super();\n    this.x = x - this.w / 2;\n    this.y = y - this.h / 2;\n    this.damage = damage;\n  }\n\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    // 淡入/淡出（:30870-30887）\n    if (this.ai0 < 180) this.alpha = Math.max(0, this.alpha - 5);\n    else {\n      this.alpha = Math.min(255, this.alpha + 5);\n      if (this.alpha >= 255) { this.dead = true; return; }\n    }\n    this.ai0++;\n    // 每 30t 掷 466（:30889-30917；单人=1 目标，CanHit 1×1 点对视线门）\n    if (this.ai0 % 30 === 0 && this.ai0 < 180) {\n      const p = game.player;\n      if (p && !p.dead) {\n        const dist = Math.hypot(p.cx - this.cx, p.cy - this.cy);\n        if (dist < 2000\n          && canHit(game.world.store, this.cx, this.cy, 1, 1, p.cx, p.cy, 1, 1)) {\n          const v83x = p.cx - this.cx, v83y = p.cy - this.cy;\n          const seed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);      // Main.rand.Next(100)\n          // Normalize(v83.RotatedByRandom(0.785))×7（:30913-30914）\n          const [rvx, rvy] = rot(v83x, v83y, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * 0.7853981852531433);\n          const d = Math.hypot(rvx, rvy) || 1;\n          const bolt = new LightningBoltProj(this.cx, this.cy,\n            (rvx / d) * 7, (rvy / d) * 7, 466, this.damage,\n            Math.atan2(v83y, v83x), seed);\n          (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (en: Entity, b: string) => void } }).entities\n            .add(bolt, 'projectiles');\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    game.addLightAt?.(this.cx, this.cy, 0.4, 0.85, 0.9);     // :30918\n    // 4 帧循环（:30919-30925）\n    if (++this.frameCounter >= 4) {\n      this.frameCounter = 0;\n      this.frame = (this.frame + 1) % 4;\n    }\n    // 尘 226 双圈（:30929-30960；速度 0 → RotatedBy(0) 不转；alpha≥150 或 ≥180t 停）\n    if (this.alpha < 150 && this.ai0 < 180) {\n      for (const radius of [this.w * 0.2, this.w * 0.6]) {\n        const a = (Math.random() - 0.5) * Math.PI * 2;       // num781 ∈ ±0.5 ×2π\n        const ox = Math.cos(a) * radius, oy = Math.sin(a) * radius;\n        const d = game.spawnDust?.(this.cx - 5 + ox, this.cy - 5 + oy, 10, 10, 226,\n          0, 0, 150, null, 0.7, true);\n        if (d && radius === this.w * 0.2) {\n          // 内圈尘离心 2（:30946）；外圈速度 0（:30958）\n          const dl = Math.hypot(d.x - this.cx, d.y - this.cy) || 1;\n          d.vx = ((d.x - this.cx) / dl) * 2;\n          d.vy = ((d.y - this.cy) / dl) * 2;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // 接触伤害（hostile；敌弹 ×2 缩放在 hitPlayer 内）\n    hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);\n    hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, 0, 'hostile');\n    hitCritters(this, game);\n  }\n\n  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    // Main.cs:32812 帧切分支：Projectile_465.png 四帧行按 frame 切片居中、alpha 渐显\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    const bright = (255 - this.alpha) / 255;\n    if (bright <= 0) return;\n    const img = projFrameImg(465, this.frame) ?? projSprite(465);\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    ctx.globalAlpha = bright;\n    if (img && img.width > 0) {\n      ctx.drawImage(img, this.cx - img.width / 2, this.cy - img.height / 2,\n        img.width, img.height);\n    } else {\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#9FE8F0';\n      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(this.cx, this.cy, 28, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n}\n\n/** 克隆体生成出口(:65646-65650 NewNPC 440:X,Y 是底部中心坐标且 Y 再 +真身 height/2;\n *  ai[3]=真身 whoAmI / localAI[1]=仪式代数)。本仓:la3=真身引用、la1=代数、跳过出生显影 */"
}

    }
  }

  /** 539 亡时 80×80 范围伤害 + 尘爆(:69366-69405) */
  private explode(game: GameHooks): void {
    this.dead = true;
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8FE8FF', 20, 3.4, { life: 40, damp: 0.94 });
    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#4FB8E8', 10, 2.2, { life: 36, damp: 0.94 });
    const p = game.player;
    if (p && !p.dead && this.dmg > 0) {
      const half = 40;                                // 80×80 居中
      if (p.x < this.cx + half && p.x + p.w > this.cx - half
        && p.y < this.cy + half && p.y + p.h > this.cy - half) {
        game.damagePlayer(this.dmg, this.cx, this.cy, null);
      }
    }
  }

  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;
    const img = projFrameImg(this.kind, this.kind === 574 ? 1 : this.frame);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(this.cx, this.cy);
    // 539 旋转分两段（AI_102）：盘旋期 vx*0.1（:32135）/冲刺期向 ToRotation+π/2 收敛
    // （:32230 AngleLerp 0.4）;574 向瞄准向 ToRotation+π/2 收敛（:32167）。此处 539 恒用
    // 盘旋档（冲刺段姿态差登记偏差）
    ctx.rotate(this.kind === 539 ? this.vx * 0.1 : Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2);
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    if (img) {
      // 原生帧 × scale=1（SetDefaults 539/574 无 scale;origin = 帧格 Size/2,Main.cs:32812
      // 列表族 :32889）。曾归一到判定盒 18×30/18×18 → 539 纵横比失真（G11）
      ctx.drawImage(img, -img.width / 2, -img.height / 2, img.width, img.height);
    } else {
      ctx.fillStyle = this.kind === 539 ? '#8FE8FF' : '#C060FF';
      ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

Wait — a bug in the SolidTiles approximation: vanilla returns true (reject) also when OUT OF RANGE (startX<0, endX>=maxX etc. → return true). My loop: `if (st.inBounds(sx,sy) && st.isSolid(sx,sy))` — out-of-bounds tiles are skipped rather than rejecting. Minor; acceptable (note: the mx,my already passed inBounds).

Hmm — but actually there's an important inversion issue: my `solid5` check treats ANY solid tile in 5×5 as reject — matches vanilla Collision.SolidTiles (returns true if any solid non-solidTop). ✓ (with the platform caveat).

Now add the VortexPortalProj import to Enemy.ts's bossAI_lunar_misc import list.
```

describe('星旋塔传送门 578/579（aiStyle 108，Projectile.cs:32505-32805）', () => {

    expect(portal.dead).toBe(true);
  });

describe('AI_089 蛾卵 478', () => {
  it('900t 孵化 → Transform 479', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    w.clock.eclipse = true;
    const h = makeHooks(w, { x: 60, y: 96 });
    const egg = spawn(h, 478, 60, 99);
    egg.y = FLOOR * TILE - egg.h;
    tick(h, 950, [egg]);
    expect(egg.vanillaId).toBe(479);
    expect(egg.maxHp).toBe(700);
  });

describe('AI_091 花岗岩元素 483', () => {
  it('通视线 → 1 直追玩家;隔墙 → 3/4 锚点寻路,均不抛异常', () => {

    const h1 = makeHooks(open, { x: 80, y: 96 });
    const e1 = spawn(h1, 483, 60, 96);
    tick(h1, 30, [e1]);
    expect(e1.ai0).toBe(1);

    const e2 = spawn(h2, 483, 60, 96);
    tick(h2, 60, [e2]);
    expect([3, 4]).toContain(e2.ai0);

});

```
  let n2 = Math.floor((y1 + h1 / 2) / 16);
  let n3 = Math.floor((x2 + w2 / 2) / 16);
  let n4 = Math.floor((y2 + h2 / 2) / 16);
  if (n1 <= 1) n1 = 1; else if (n1 >= st.w) n1 = st.w - 1;
  if (n3 <= 1) n3 = 1; else if (n3 >= st.w) n3 = st.w - 1;
  if (n2 <= 1) n2 = 1; else if (n2 >= st.h - 40) n2 = st.h - 40;
  if (n4 <= 1) n4 = 1; else if (n4 >= st.h - 40) n4 = st.h - 40;
  return [n1, n2, n3, n4];
}

/** Collision.CanHit（cs:388-480）：起点矩形左上角像素坐标 + 尺寸 → 目标同构 */
export function canHit(st: Store, x1: number, y1: number, w1: number, h1: number,
  x2: number, y2: number, w2: number, h2: number): boolean {
  let [num, num2, num3, num4] = centerTiles(st, x1, y1, w1, h1, x2, y2, w2, h2);
  for (;;) {
    const d5 = Math.abs(num - num3), d6 = Math.abs(num2 - num4);
    if (num === num3 && num2 === num4) return true;
    if (d5 > d6) {
      num += num >= num3 ? -1 : 1;
      if (isNull(st, num, num2 - 1) || isNull(st, num, num2 + 1)) return false;
      if (fullSolid(st, num, num2 - 1) && fullSolid(st, num, num2 + 1)) return false;
    } else {
      num2 += num2 >= num4 ? -1 : 1;
      if (isNull(st, num - 1, num2) || isNull(st, num + 1, num2)) return false;
      if (fullSolid(st, num - 1, num2) && fullSolid(st, num + 1, num2)) return false;
    }
    if (isNull(st, num, num2)) return false;
    if (!passable(st, num, num2)) return false;                // 落脚格整实心 → 阻挡
  }
}

/** Collision.CanHitLine（cs:590-785）：分数步进逐格扫描，ANY 整实心即阻挡 */
export function canHitLine(st: Store, x1: number, y1: number, w1: number, h1: number,
  x2: number, y2: number, w2: number, h2: number): boolean {
  const [num, num2, num3, num4] = centerTiles(st, x1, y1, w1, h1, x2, y2, w2, h2);
  const d5 = Math.abs(num - num3), d6 = Math.abs(num2 - num4);
  if (d5 === 0 && d6 === 0) return true;
  let stepX = 1, stepY = 1;                                   // num7/num8
  if (d5 === 0) stepX = 0;
  else if (d6 === 0) stepY = 0;
  else if (d5 > d6) stepX = d5 / d6;
  else stepY = d6 / d5;
  let accX = 0, accY = 0;                                     // num9/num10
  let phase = num2 < num4 ? 2 : 1;                            // num11：2=先行 X 步
  let remX = Math.floor(d5), remY = Math.floor(d6);           // num12/num13
  const sx = Math.sign(num3 - num), sy = Math.sign(num4 - num2);
  let cx = num, cy = num2;
  let done = false, lastSingle = false;                       // flag / flag2
  for (;;) {
    if (phase === 2) {
      accX += stepX;
      const steps = Math.floor(accX);
      accX -= steps;
      for (let j = 0; j < steps; j++) {
        if (isNull(st, cx, cy - 1) || isNull(st, cx, cy) || isNull(st, cx, cy + 1)) return false;
        if (plainSolid(st, cx, cy - 1) || plainSolid(st, cx, cy + 1) || plainSolid(st, cx, cy)) return false;
        if (remX === 0 && remY === 0) { done = true; break; }
        cx += sx; remX--;
        if (remX === 0 && remY === 0 && steps === 1) lastSingle = true;
      }
      if (remY !== 0) phase = 1;
    } else {
      accY += stepY;
      const steps = Math.floor(accY);
      accY -= steps;
      for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
        if (isNull(st, cx - 1, cy) || isNull(st, cx, cy) || isNull(st, cx + 1, cy)) return false;
        if (plainSolid(st, cx - 1, cy) || plainSolid(st, cx + 1, cy) || plainSolid(st, cx, cy)) return false;
        if (remX === 0 && remY === 0) { done = true; break; }
        cy += sy; remY--;
        if (remX === 0 && remY === 0 && steps === 1) lastSingle = true;
      }
      if (remX !== 0) phase = 2;
    }
    if (isNull(st, cx, cy)) return false;
    if (plainSolid(st, cx, cy)) return false;
    if (done || lastSingle) return true;
  }
}

```
// Collision.CanHit / CanHitLine 1:1（Terarria1456, 1.4.5.6）：
//   CanHit     Collision.cs:388-480（Point 重载，实体矩形 → 中心 tile 起止）
//   CanHitLine Collision.cs:590-785（分数步进 Bresenham，三 tile 一组 ANY 判阻挡）
//
// 逐 tile 扫描（替换旧的 8px 采样近似），关键语义：
//  - 阻挡 tile = active && !inActive(致动) && tileSolid[type] && !tileSolidTop[type]
//    （CanHit 额外要求 slope==0 && !halfBrick；CanHitLine 不查半砖/坡面——原文如此）
//  - 半砖/坡面/平台(solidTop)/已致动 tile 一律不阻挡视线；
//    开门态是独立 tile id（tileSolid=false），天然不阻挡。
//  - CanHit：沿主轴逐格走，"上下(或左右)两邻格均为整块实心"= 被夹住 → false；
//    落脚格本身可穿才继续走，落脚格为整块实心即出循环 → false。
//  - CanHitLine：每步检查 当前+两邻格 ANY 整实心 → false；步末落脚格实心 → false。
//  - 起止 tile 钳制：x∈[1,maxTilesX-1]、y∈[1,maxTilesY-40]；越界 tile 视同 null → false。
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import type { TileStore } from '../world/TileStore';
import { TileStore as TS } from '../world/TileStore';

type Store = Pick<TileStore, 'w' | 'h' | 'type' | 'wire' | 'half' | 'slope' | 'inBounds' | 'idx'>;

/** tile == null（未加载/越界）→ 调用方 return false */
function isNull(st: Store, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  return !st.inBounds(x, y);
}

/** CanHit 的整块实心判定（Collision.cs:443-447 / :449-453 条件串）：
 *  active && !inActive && tileSolid && !tileSolidTop && slope==0 && !halfBrick */
function fullSolid(st: Store, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const t = st.type[i];
  if (t === 0) return false;                                   // !active()
  if (st.wire[i] & TS.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false;             // inActive()
  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && st.slope[i] === 0 && st.half[i] === 0;
}

/** CanHitLine 的整块实心判定（不查 slope/halfBrick，cs:707-709） */
function plainSolid(st: Store, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const t = st.type[i];
  if (t === 0) return false;
  if (st.wire[i] & TS.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false;
  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform;
}

/** CanHit 循环条件取反：当前格"可穿"才继续走（cs:477） */
function passable(st: Store, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const t = st.type[i];
  if (st.wire[i] & TS.WIRE_ACTUATED) return true;              // inActive → 可穿
  if (t === 0) return true;                                    // !active → 可穿
  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
  return !d || !d.solid || !!d.platform;                       // !tileSolid || tileSolidTop
}

/** 实体中心 tile 起止 + 原版钳制（cs:390-431） */
function centerTiles(st: Store, x1: number, y1: number, w1: number, h1: number,
  x2: number, y2: number, w2: number, h2: number): [number, number, number, number] {
  let n1 = Math.floor((x1 + w1 / 2) / 16);
  let n2 = Math.floor((y1 + h1 / 2) / 16);

```

y < 60; y++) walledWorld.store.setTile(70, y, 1);   // x=70 隔墙\n    const walled = makeHooks(walledWorld, { x: 80, y: 48 });\n    const tower = spawn(walled, 422, 60, 90);\n    tower.bInit = true;\n    tick(walled, 1, [tower]);\n    const portals = walled.projectiles.filter((p) => p instanceof VortexPortalProj) as VortexPortalProj[];\n    expect(portals.length).toBe(1);   // 玩家飞到被钳制塔(y≈430)400px 内\n    const tower = spawn(h, 493, 80, 80);\n    tower.bInit = true;\n    tick(h, 1, [tower]);\n    const marks = h.projectiles.filter((p) => p instanceof StardustMarkProj) as StardustMarkProj[];\n    expect(marks.length).toBeGreaterThan(0); 84	    damage: number, rotBase: number, seed: number) {
85	    super();
86	    this.x = x - this.w / 2; this.y = y - this.h / 2;
87	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
88	    this.projId = projId;
89	    this.damage = damage;
90	    this.ai0 = rotBase;
91	    this.ai1 = seed;
92	  }
93	
94	  /** oldPos 记录（:15762-15768：frameCounter==0 时移位入环） */
95	  private recordStep(): void {
96	    if (this.x === this.lastRecX && this.y === this.lastRecY) this.stationarySteps++;
97	    else this.stationarySteps = 1;
98	    this.lastRecX = this.x; this.lastRecY = this.y;
99	    this.trail.push(this.x, this.y);
100	    if (this.trail.length > 20) this.trail.splice(0, this.trail.length - 20);   // 10 点
101	  }
102	
103	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
104	    // extraUpdates 子步循环（:15331-15336）：AI/位移/命中逐子步
105	    for (let u = 0; u <= this.extraUpdates; u++) {
106	      this.subStep(game);
107	      if (this.dead) return;
108	    }
109	  }
110	
111	  private subStep(game: GameHooks): void {
112	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
113	    // 580 哨兵（:31061-31065）
114	    if (this.projId === 580 && this.lai1 === 0 && this.ai0 >= 900) {
115	      this.ai0 -= 1000;
116	      this.lai1 = -1;
117	    }
118	    this.frameCounter++;
119	    game.addLightAt?.(this.cx, this.cy, 0.3, 0.45, 0.5);   // :31068/:30946
120	    if (this.vx === 0 && this.vy === 0) {
121	      // ---- 静止相（:31086-31025 / :31144-31134 同构）----
122	      if (this.frameCounter >= this.extraUpdates * 2) {
123	        this.frameCounter = 0;
124	        this.recordStep();
125	        // oldPos 全等 → Kill（:31091-31099；记录满 10 点全静止即等价）
126	        if (this.stationarySteps >= 10) { this.dead = true; return; }
127	        // 尘门 1/4 × (localAI1==2 ? 1/2 : 1/6)（:31100/:31009）
128	        if (Math.floor(Math.random() * this.extraUpdates) === 0
129	          && Math.floor(Math.random() * (this.lai1 === 2 ? 2 : 6)) === 0) {
130	          for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
131	            // rotation ± π/2 随机侧向、速 1..1.8（:31102-31109/:31011-31018）
132	            const a = this.rot + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1) * (Math.PI / 2);
133	            const len = Math.random() * 0.8 + 1;
134	            game.spawnDust?.(this.cx, this.cy, 0, 0, 226,
135	              Math.cos(a) * len, Math.sin(a) * len, 0, null, 1.2, true);
136	          }
137	          if (Math.floor(Math.random() * 5) === 0) {
138	            // 尘 31 上升火花（:31112-31118；静止相 velocity=0 → 偏移 0）
139	            const d = game.spawnDust?.(this.cx - 4, this.cy - 4, 8, 8, 31,
140	              0, 0, 100, null, 1.5);
141	            if (d) { d.vx *= 0.5; d.vy = -Math.abs(d.vy); }
142	          }
143	        }
144	      }
145	    } else {
146	      // ---- 飞行相（:31017-31052 / :31088 起 466 同构）----
147	      if (this.frameCounter >= this.extraUpdates * 2) {
148	        this.frameCounter = 0;
149	        const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
150	        const ur = new UnifiedRandom(this.ai1 | 0);        // (int)ai[1]（:31024）
151	        let attempts = 0;
152	        let pickX = 0, pickY = -1;
153	        let settled = false;
154	        for (;;) {
155	          let next = ur.next();
156	          this.ai1 = next;                                  // :31029 覆写种子
157	          next %= 100;
158	          const f = (next / 100) * Math.PI * 2;
159	          let cx = Math.cos(f), cy = Math.sin(f);
160	          if (cy > 0) cy *= -1;                             // 只取向上半（:31036-31039）
161	          let fail = false;
162	          if (cy > -0.02) fail = true;                      // :31040-31043 近水平弃
163	          const budget = cx * (this.extraUpdates + 1) * 2 * spd + this.lai0;
164	          if (budget > 40 || budget < -40) fail = true;     // :31044-31049 X 预算
165	          if (fail) {
166	            // 100 次无解 → 停摆（466 localAI1=1 :31050-31052 / 580 +=2 :31174-31177）
167	            if (++attempts >= 100) {
168	              this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
169	              if (this.projId === 466) this.lai1 = 1;
170	              else if (this.lai1 < 1) this.lai1 += 2;
171	              settled = true;
172	              break;
173	            }
174	            continue;
175	          }
176	          pickX = cx; pickY = cy;
177	          break;
178	        }
179	        if (!settled) {
180	          this.lai0 += pickX * (this.extraUpdates + 1) * 2 * spd;   // :31045
181	          // velocity = pick.RotatedBy(ai[0]+π/2) × spd（:31046-31048）
182	          const a = this.ai0 + Math.PI / 2;
183	          const c = Math.cos(a), s = Math.sin(a);
184	          this.vx = (pickX * c - pickY * s) * spd;
185	          this.vy = (pickX * s + pickY * c) * spd;
186	          this.rot = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI / 2;
187	        }
188	        this.recordStep();
189	      }
190	      // 引擎位移（UpdatePosition :19026 逐子步）
191	      this.x += this.vx;
192	      this.y += this.vy;
193	    }
194	    // 撞块（tileCollide=true；HandleMovement 撞实心 → :16896-16903 停摆不消亡）
195	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
196	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
197	    const st = game.world.store;
198	    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
199	    if (st.get(tx, ty) !== 0) {
200	      game.cutTile?.(tx, ty);
201	      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty) && this.lai1 < 1) {
202	        this.lai1 += 2;
203	        this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
204	      }
205	    }
206	    // 命中玩家：本体 ∪ oldPos 轨迹逐点矩形（Colliding :14733-14744；敌弹不消耗）
207	    hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);
208	    for (let i = 0; i < this.trail.length; i += 2) {
209	      const px = this.trail[i], py = this.trail[i + 1];
210	      if (px === 0 && py === 0) continue;                  // Zero 槽跳过（:14735）
211	      if (hitPlayer({ x: px, y: py, w: this.w, h: this.h }, game, this.damage)) break;
212	    }
213	    hitTownNpcs(this, game, this.damage, 0, 'hostile');
214	    hitCritters(this, game);
215	  }
216	
217	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
218	    // Main.cs:33678-33760：沿 oldPos 逐段 DrawLaser(Extra_33)。466 三层 / 580 两层
219	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
220	    if (!ctx) return;
221	    const tex = extra33();
222	    // 折线点列：oldPos（最老→最新，Zero 槽跳过）→ 本体中心（:33705 oldPos[0]→end 段）
223	    const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
224	    for (let i = 0; i < this.trail.length; i += 2) {
225	      if (this.trail[i] === 0 && this.trail[i + 1] === 0) continue;
226	      pts.push([this.trail[i] + this.w / 2, this.trail[i + 1] + this.h / 2]);
227	    }
228	    pts.push([this.cx, this.cy]);
229	    const passes: Array<{ w: number; col: string }> = this.projId === 466
230	      ? [{ w: 0.6, col: 'rgba(115,204,219,0.5)' },
231	        { w: 0.4, col: 'rgba(113,251,255,0.5)' },
232	        { w: 0.2, col: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)' }]

234	      : [{ w: this.lai1 === -1 || this.lai1 === 1 ? 0.3 : 0.5, col: 'rgba(115,244,219,0.5)' },
235	        { w: this.lai1 === -1 || this.lai1 === 1 ? 0.1 : 0.3, col: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)' }];
236	    ctx.save();
237	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
238	    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
239	    for (let i = 0; i + 1 < pts.length; i++) {
240	      const [x1, y1] = pts[i], [x2, y2] = pts[i + 1];
241	      const len = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
242	      if (len < 0.5) continue;
243	      const ang = Math.atan2(y2 - y1, x2 - x1);
244	      for (const p of passes) {
245	        ctx.save();
246	        ctx.translate(x1, y1);
247	        ctx.rotate(ang);
248	        if (tex && tex.width > 0) {
249	          const bw = BAND_W * p.w;
250	          ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, BAND_Y, BAND_W, BAND_H,
251	            -bw / 2, 0, bw, len);   // 体带沿段拉伸（LightningLaserDraw stage1 近似）
252	        } else {
253	          ctx.strokeStyle = p.col;
254	          ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 14 * p.w * 0.3);
255	          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(len, 0); ctx.stroke();
256	        }
257	        ctx.restore();
258	      }
259	    }
260	    ctx.restore();
261	  }
262	}

//   ATL-07 vanilla.json sheet 集 ↔ PNG 文件集对称差(孤儿图/缺表,info)

import { readFileSync, existsSync, openSync, readSync, closeSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');

/** PNG IHDR 尺寸(读 24 字节,不整文件读) */
function pngSize(file) {
  const fd = openSync(file, 'r');
  const b = Buffer.alloc(24);
  try { readSync(fd, b, 0, 24, 0); } finally { closeSync(fd); }
  if (b[0] !== 0x89 || b[1] !== 0x50 || b[2] !== 0x4e || b[3] !== 0x47) return null; // 签名
  return { w: b.readUInt32BE(16), h: b.readUInt32BE(20) };
